独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38001009392 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

⌄

反馈证据核对

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    E4 的 `case 'image'` 直接渲染 `<img src={url}>`，没有 onError 或回退；E5 显示同一文件的 OfficePreview 会用 DownloadFallback 与 officeFailed 文案处理失败，说明失败反馈在组件集内并不一致。应用因此 fork file-preview-content.tsx 增加图片失败提示（E32）。

    实际影响
    MIME 正确但内容损坏的图片在业务界面显示为坏图，用户与 QA 会把“损坏文件”误判为“预览成功但没有内容”，增加排查成本；应用侧绕行使上游组件与本地副本出现分叉。
    建议改法
    在 @nocobase/app-plugin-file 的 registry 图片预览分支复用与 OfficePreview 一致的失败模式：为 <img> 增加 onError，渲染 FileThumbnail 加 files.imageFailed 文案。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    上传或播种一个 MIME 声明为 image/* 但字节损坏的文件并打开其预览
    应有能力 / 行为
    预览区域给出明确的图片加载失败提示，或至少与 OfficePreview 一样可感知失败
    实际观察
    registry 组件的 image 分支只渲染裸 <img>，失败时浏览器显示坏图图标，无文案与回退
    应用绕行与代价
    应用 fork file-preview-content.tsx，为图片增加 onError 状态并渲染失败提示
    改进后的验收标准
    损坏图片的预览出现明确失败文案/缩略图回退，正常图片仍以内联预览显示；对应组件单测覆盖 image onError 分支

      if (!url && !error)

    app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:96–128E32

    应用 fork 的 ImagePreview 用 useState(failed) 捕获 onError，失败时渲染 FileThumbnail 与 files.imageFailed 文案，注释说明浏览器 onError 是唯一可用信号。

    function ImagePreview(inputProps: {
  readonly file: FileRecord;
  readonly url?: string;
}): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation('@nocobase/app-plugin-file');
  const { file, url } = inputProps;
  // A file can carry an image MIME type and still be damaged. The browser's
  // `onError` is the only signal available, so the failure is shown here rather
  // than leaving a broken image that looks like a successful preview.
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  if (failed) {
    return (
      <div role='alert' className='flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-8'>
        <div className='h-24 w-24'>
          <FileThumbnail file={file} />
        </div>
        <p>
          {t('files.imageFailed', {
            defaultValue:
              'This image is damaged or cannot be displayed in the browser.',
          })}
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <img
      src={url}
      alt={file.filename}
      className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
    上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
    ### [轻微][能力缺口] 注册表图片预览器对损坏图片没有失败路径，应用只能 fork 组件补 onError

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-file`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

E4 的 `case 'image'` 直接渲染 `<img src={url}>`，没有 onError 或回退；E5 显示同一文件的 OfficePreview 会用 DownloadFallback 与 officeFailed 文案处理失败，说明失败反馈在组件集内并不一致。应用因此 fork file-preview-content.tsx 增加图片失败提示（E32）。

**影响**：MIME 正确但内容损坏的图片在业务界面显示为坏图，用户与 QA 会把“损坏文件”误判为“预览成功但没有内容”，增加排查成本；应用侧绕行使上游组件与本地副本出现分叉。

**建议改动**：在 @nocobase/app-plugin-file 的 registry 图片预览分支复用与 OfficePreview 一致的失败模式：为 <img> 增加 onError，渲染 FileThumbnail 加 files.imageFailed 文案。

**验收标准**：损坏图片的预览出现明确失败文案/缩略图回退，正常图片仍以内联预览显示；对应组件单测覆盖 image onError 分支

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:71–79` — PreviewContent 的 switch 在 case 'image' 直接返回 <img src={url} ...>，没有 onError、失败状态或回退 UI。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:159–178` — OfficePreview 在无 embedUrl 或 failed 时返回 DownloadFallback，并给出 officeFailed / officeUrl 文案，显示同类组件具有失败反馈路径。

    F7 · 贡献路由自带的 onError 不会随 api.route('/') 生效，server-routes 指引未提示

    文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development · 原评审：未解决

    ⌄

    反馈证据核对

    定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

      反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

      E9 的应用层 api 路由在 `api.route('/', router)` 之前注册了全局 onError；E13 的 server-routes.md 建议把 router 抽出来再挂载，但未说明贡献 router 自己的 onError 在挂载后不再生效、需由应用层统一处理。E36 的注释显示应用注意到了这一点。

      实际影响
      若 Agent 按指引在贡献 router 内写 onError 并期望它生效，错误会被应用层 onError 抢先处理，错误体可能不一致而难以定位。
      建议改法
      在 server-routes.md 的“抽取 router 再挂载”段落补充：贡献 router 的 onError 会被装配层 onError 取代，错误处理应集中在应用层或使用 ApiError。
      诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
      触发条件
      在 defineApiRoutes 贡献 router 内注册 onError，再通过应用层 api.route('/') 挂载
      应有能力 / 行为
      指引说明 onError 的生效层级，Agent 知道错误处理应放在哪一层
      实际观察
      指引只给出抽取 router 的示例，未说明贡献 router 的 onError 在挂载后不再生效
      应用绕行与代价
      应用在应用层集中注册 onError（E36 注释已体现）
      改进后的验收标准
      按更新后的指引放置 onError 能得到预期的统一错误体；示例中标注 onError 的归属层级

      应用层 api router 在装配前注册全局 onError，通过 api.route('/', router) 挂载贡献 router，并在注册期做重复路由断言。

        api.use('*', requestIdMiddleware());
        // The `api` section's global limits, each installed only when configured.
        installApiLimits(api, this.config.get('api'));
        // The last resort: anything no router recognized is an unexpected failure, answered with an opaque 500.
        api.onError((error, context) => apiErrorHandler(toApiError(error), context));
        const apiRouters = [];
        const roots = [];
        for (const [index, { contribution, owner }] of this.routes.entries()) {
            const router = await contribution.createRouter(this);
            if (contribution.scope === 'api') {
                apiRouters.push({
                    owner: owner ?? `route contribution #${index + 1}`,
                    router,
                });
            }
            else
                roots.push(router);
        }
        // The API documentation, mounted with the contributions so a plugin route under `/swagger` fails start as a
        // duplicate rather than shadowing it.
        const apiDocs = this.container.has(apiDocsToken)
            ? this.container.resolve(apiDocsToken)
            : undefined;
        if (apiDocs) {
            apiRouters.push({
                owner: '@nocobase/app-server',
                router: createApiDocsRouter(apiDocs),
            });

        // Routers a runtime dispatcher forwards to are checked with the rest, at the paths they answer below `/api`. Only
        // those registered by now, during boot, are seen; they are checked here and never mounted.
        const forwardedRouters = (apiDocs?.forwardedApiRoutes.routers ?? []).map((registration) => ({
            owner: registration.owner,
            router: registration.router,
            mount: registration.prefix.slice('/api'.length),
            // A route outside the forwarded path is never reached, so it cannot shadow anything.
            includes: (path) => isForwardedTo(registration, `/api${path}`),
        }));
        // Hono lets the first matching route win without a word, so a second contribution answering the same method and
        // path would be dead code nobody notices. Checked before anything mounts, so a failed start leaves no half-built
        // router behind.
        assertNoDuplicateApiRoutes([...apiRouters, ...forwardedRouters]);
        for (const { router } of apiRouters)
            api.route('/', router);
        api.all('*', apiNotFoundHandler);
        this.router.route('/api', api);
        for (const router of roots)
      app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:205–234E13

      给出把 Hono router 抽成模块并用 defineApiRoutes 挂载的完整示例，说明路由工厂通过 app.container 解析依赖。

      One or two handlers belong directly in the factory. When a domain grows several handlers or shared error handling, extract a function that returns its own `Hono` and mount it:

```ts
export function createOrderRoutes(options: CreateOrderRoutesOptions): Hono {
  const routes = new Hono();
  routes.use('*', options.auth.required());
  routes.get(
    '/:orderId',
    describeRoute({
      tags: ['Orders'],
      summary: 'Get an order',
      operationId: 'getOrder',
      responses: {
        '200': dataResponse(OrderSchema),
        '401': apiErrorResponse(401),
        '404': apiErrorResponse(404),
        '500': apiErrorResponse(500),
      },
    }),
    apiValidator('param', OrderParams),
    async (context) =>
      context.json({
        data: await options.orders.get(context.req.valid('param').orderId),
      }),
  );
  return routes;
}
```

Do not write a helper that mutates a router passed in by its caller. Returning a router you own keeps the security boundary inside the thing being tested.
      app/server/routes/project-materials.ts:55–120E36

      应用用 createProjectMaterialsRouter 组合 describeRoute、apiValidator 与 ApiError 实现材料 CRUD，显示 app-server 错误/校验契约的实际接入。

       * Runs a service write, translating a validation refusal into the standard API
 * error.
 *
 * The translation happens here rather than in `router.onError`, because the
 * application mounts every contribution with `api.route('/', router)`, which
 * merges routes into one router and drops a mounted router's own `onError`.
 * Converting before the error escapes keeps the refusal a `400`.
 */
async function runWrite<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await operation();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ProjectMaterialError) {
      throw toProjectMaterialApiError(error);
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

export function createProjectMaterialsRouter(app: Application): Hono {
  const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
  const service = app.container.resolve<ProjectMaterialsService>(
    projectMaterialsServiceToken,
  );
  const router = new Hono();

  router.get(
    '/projectMaterials',
    auth.required(),
    describeRoute({
      tags: TAGS,
      summary: 'List project materials',
      operationId: 'listProjectMaterials',
      description:
        'Lists the signed-in user’s own materials, most recently updated first, each with its attachments. Another user’s materials are never included.',
      responses: {
        '200': listResponse(
          ProjectMaterialSchema,
          undefined,
          'One page of materials.',
        ),
        '401': apiErrorResponse(401),
        '500': apiErrorResponse(500),
      },
    }),
    apiValidator('query', ListProjectMaterialsQuery),
    async (context) => {
      const { page = 1, pageSize = 20 } = context.req.valid('query');
      const { items, total } = await service.list(currentUserId(context), {
        page,
        pageSize,
      });
      return context.json({ data: items, meta: { page, pageSize, total } });
    },
  );

      tags: TAGS,
      summary: 'Get a project material',

      上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
      ### [轻微][指引缺失] 贡献路由自带的 onError 不会随 api.route('/') 生效，server-routes 指引未提示

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-server`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.0）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

E9 的应用层 api 路由在 `api.route('/', router)` 之前注册了全局 onError；E13 的 server-routes.md 建议把 router 抽出来再挂载，但未说明贡献 router 自己的 onError 在挂载后不再生效、需由应用层统一处理。E36 的注释显示应用注意到了这一点。

**影响**：若 Agent 按指引在贡献 router 内写 onError 并期望它生效，错误会被应用层 onError 抢先处理，错误体可能不一致而难以定位。

**建议改动**：在 server-routes.md 的“抽取 router 再挂载”段落补充：贡献 router 的 onError 会被装配层 onError 取代，错误处理应集中在应用层或使用 ApiError。

**验收标准**：按更新后的指引放置 onError 能得到预期的统一错误体；示例中标注 onError 的归属层级

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:255–302` — 应用层 api router 在装配前注册全局 onError，通过 api.route('/', router) 挂载贡献 router，并在注册期做重复路由断言。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:205–234` — 给出把 Hono router 抽成模块并用 defineApiRoutes 挂载的完整示例，说明路由工厂通过 app.container 解析依赖。
- `app/server/routes/project-materials.ts:55–120` — 应用用 createProjectMaterialsRouter 组合 describeRoute、apiValidator 与 ApiError 实现材料 CRUD，显示 app-server 错误/校验契约的实际接入。

        反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

        E15 的 QueryAdapter.select() 签名把返回的 TResult 覆盖为 Row；因此 `.select('id').executeTakeFirst()` 得到 unknown，而不是 { id: string }。E33 显示应用在种子里据此补了 `<{ id: string }>` 才通过类型检查。

        packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/query/types.d.ts:86–90E15

        QueryAdapter.select() 的签名把 TResult 覆盖为 Row，并说明传入选择列后返回类型随之改变；这使 select('id').executeTakeFirst() 得到 Row/unknown 而非所选列类型。

        export interface SelectQuery<TRecord extends Row = Row, TResult extends Row = TRecord> {
    select(selection: SelectionExpression): SelectQuery<TRecord, Row>;
    select(selections: readonly SelectionExpression[]): SelectQuery<TRecord, Row>;
    select(factory: SelectionFactory): SelectQuery<TRecord, Row>;
    selectAll(table?: string): SelectQuery<TRecord, Row>;
        packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/seed/types.d.ts:13–31E16

        SeedContext 暴露 config、container、repository、query、connection；defineSeed 支持 transaction 为 true/false/auto，说明 seed 可经 container 解析应用服务。

        export interface SeedContext {
    readonly config: DatabaseTaskConfig;
    readonly container: ServiceResolver;
    /**
     * The default tool for installation data, bound to the connection this seed
     * runs on — the transaction's connection when it runs in one.
     *
     * Installation data is written in Collection terms: logical field names,
     * relations and nested writes, with field encoding and timestamps handled
     * for the dialect rather than by each seed.
     */
    repository<TRecord extends object = RepositoryRecord, TCreate extends object = Partial<TRecord>, TUpdate extends object = Partial<TRecord>>(collection: string): Repository<TRecord, TCreate, TUpdate>;
    /**
     * Row-level access for what `repository` cannot express, such as a read
     * against a physical table that backs no Collection.
     */
    readonly query: QueryAdapter;
    readonly connection: SeedConnection;
}

        ### [轻微][易用性改进] QueryAdapter.select() 把 TResult 收窄为 Row，select('id') 的种子代码回退为 unknown

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

E15 的 QueryAdapter.select() 签名把返回的 TResult 覆盖为 Row；因此 `.select('id').executeTakeFirst()` 得到 unknown，而不是 { id: string }。E33 显示应用在种子里据此补了 `<{ id: string }>` 才通过类型检查。

**影响**：按最自然的写法查询单列会触发类型错误，Agent 需要一次编译/修复周期；应用侧虽可显式泛型修复，但属于可避免的摩擦。

**验收标准**：不写显式泛型的 select('id').executeTakeFirst() 可直接访问 id；类型测试或编译用例覆盖该路径

        F8 · 文件内容路由默认公开，每个私有文件应用都要自行包裹一次认证

        NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file · 原评审：未解决

        ⌄

        反馈证据核对

        定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

          反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

          E1/E2 显示内容字节路由默认公开，E3 的 Skill 要求应用在 <accessPath>/* 上自行安装 auth.required()。这虽为文档化的既定契约，但对以私有文件为主的场景，重复的手工包裹是常见易漏点（漏一次即为未授权访问）。

          实际影响
          应用必须记住对每个文件内容路由加认证，遗漏会导致私有文件可被匿名读取；当前只有 Skill 文案兜底，没有框架层可声明开关。
          建议改法
          考虑为 content 路由提供显式选项（如 authenticate: true / protectedPath 回调），或提供可复用的“前缀认证包装器”，把重复的 app-owned auth 封装为一次性接入。
          诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
          触发条件
          应用接入文件内容路由并需要私有访问
          应有能力 / 行为
          有一个声明式或可复用的入口表达“该内容路由需认证”
          实际观察
          内容路由默认公开，应用需自行构造外部 Hono 并在 <accessPath>/* 安装 auth.required()
          应用绕行与代价
          应用用 wrapApiContribution/wrapRootContribution 手工包裹（E30/E31）
          改进后的验收标准
          应用以一句声明或一个包装器即可保护内容路由；未声明时保持现有公开契约并在 Skill 中说明
          评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
          packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:26–35E1

          defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes 的注释写明“Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned.”，并把文件元数据 API 交由 defineRepositoryApiRoutes 生成。

          /** Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned. */
export function defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes(options) {
    const paths = [];
    const entries = options.repositories.map((entry) => {
        const accessPath = normalizeAccessPath(entry.accessPath ?? `/uploads/${entry.name}`);
        if (paths.some((path) => path === accessPath ||
            path.startsWith(`${accessPath}/`) ||
            accessPath.startsWith(`${path}/`)))
            throw new Error(`Conflicting file accessPath: ${accessPath}`);
        paths.push(accessPath);
          packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:198–224E2

          内容字节路由 router.get(accessPath/:file) 通过 findOne({ filter: { id } }) 取文件并流式返回附件，不依赖调用者身份，即内容路由默认公开；响应带 no-store、X-Content-Type-Options 与 content-disposition。

                          router.get(`${entry.accessPath}/:file`, async (c) => {
                    const match = /^([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})(?:\.([a-z0-9]{1,32}))?$/.exec(c.req.param('file'));
                    if (!match)
                        return c.notFound();
                    const record = await files.findOne({ filter: { id: match[1] } });
                    if (!record || record.ext !== (match[2] ?? ''))
                        return c.notFound();
                    const disk = app.container
                        .resolve(driveManagerToken)
                        .use(record.disk);
                    if (!(await disk.exists(record.key)))
                        return c.notFound();
                    c.header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store');
                    if (entry.accessMode === 'redirect') {
                        const url = await files.getStorageUrl(record);
                        const publicBasePath = app.publicBasePath ?? '';
                        // Check the final target after the host rewrites root-relative redirects.
                        const location = new URL(addBasePathToLocation(url, publicBasePath), c.req.url);
                        if (location.origin === new URL(c.req.url).origin &&
                            entries.some((item) => location.pathname.startsWith(`${joinBasePath(publicBasePath, item.accessPath)}/`)))
                            throw new FileRepositoryError('STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE', 'Storage URL points back to a file access route.');
                        return c.redirect(url, 302);
                    }
                    c.header('Content-Type', validMime(record.mimeType));
                    c.header('Content-Length', String(record.size));
                    c.header('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
                    c.header('Content-Security-Policy', "sandbox; default-src 'none'");
          app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:124–128E3

          明确写出“The content route under accessPath is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.”，并给出受限文件配方：应用自行在 <accessPath>/* 安装 auth.required()，且禁止对全部路径使用 router.use('*')。

          An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

          ### [轻微][易用性改进] 文件内容路由默认公开，每个私有文件应用都要自行包裹一次认证

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-file`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

          实现者过程与运行背景 · 0 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

          本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

          本轮过程记录

          本轮修复 verify 的 test 失败：客户端资料功能引用的 18 个 materials.* 翻译键未登记，导致 app-locale-coverage.test.ts 的中英文用例同时失败。已在 client/locales/en-US.ts 与 zh-CN.ts 的 materials 命名空间补齐全部键，并顺带修正资料详情在无权访问时仍显示 Edit/Delete 的页脚。另确认此前 B02 的 DOCX 预览“卡住”是本地 agent-browser 缺少 module worker 兼容脚本造成的假失败，工厂浏览器阶段已导出该脚本，应用无需改动。此前轮次的 seed 类型、schemas 格式与 Hono Context 类型修复保留，功能与测试未回滚。

          实现者未记录具体阻塞，不等于本轮没有问题。

          实现者补充建议

          来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

          建议 01 · 验收机制

          format:auto 的规范化未在后续 format:check 中体现

          可自动化

          verify-3 的 format:auto 报告已写入 server/routes/schemas.ts（无 (unchanged) 标记），但紧接着的 format:check 仍判定该文件未格式化。手工执行同一 format-changes.mjs 后，同一 format:check 通过。说明应用源字节仍是最终检查的依据，最终验收若不重跑 format:auto，格式必须由源自身满足。

          建议改动

          在最终验收阶段同样先执行 format:auto，或在 repair 工作区交接/封存补丁前固化其输出，使修复验证与最终验证使用同一份已规范化字节。

          来源：Agent · retro.json

          建议 02 · 验收机制

          本地 agent-browser 冒烟必须注入工厂的 module worker 兼容脚本

          可自动化

          不设置 AGENT_BROWSER_INIT_SCRIPTS 时，agent-browser 的 module worker 域名守卫会在 await import(url) 期间丢弃应用 worker 的初始 postMessage；@silurus/ooxml 的解析 worker 立即发送 init/parse，消息被丢弃后 DocxDocument.load 以 'worker request timed out after 5000ms' 拒绝，表现为 DOCX 预览永久卡住（B02 假失败）。探针证实：带 {type:'module'} 的 blob/data/asset worker 在立即 postMessage 时丢消息，延迟约 800ms 则正常，classic worker 不受影响。设置 AGENT_BROWSER_INIT_SCRIPTS=<workspace>/.github/scripts/browser-worker-compat.js 后四个立即发送消息的探针全部通过，DOCX 内联预览渲染出 canvas 正文。工厂的 browser-acceptance.sh 已导出该变量，因此无需改应用代码。

          建议改动

          把该环境变量固定进本地冒烟文档或 agent-browser 调用封装（与 browser-acceptance.sh 保持一致），避免每个本地复现的人重新踩一次 module worker 丢消息的假失败。

          来源：Agent · retro.json

          查看原始复盘数据

          本次评审的覆盖限制
          • 本次评审的模型不支持图像输入，无法查看任何 PNG 截图；ui.status=not-reviewed，跨页面样式一致性与组件/主题指引有效性未评估，第一轮截图不能作为最终 UI 结论。
          • 未运行应用、未安装依赖、未复现浏览器行为；所有可靠性结论来自冻结的 dist 源码、Skill 文档与 agent-history 日志。
          • 未覆盖文件模块的多文件字段、签名 URL、分片上传、对象存储等路径；未覆盖 PDF/文本/未知 MIME 的预览。
          • 未覆盖 db 的事务模式、回滚与多数据源；未运行数据库，select() 类型摩擦来自冻结 d.ts 与应用侧修复痕迹。
          • 未覆盖 authentication 的注册/改密/部署等生命周期全文；未实测 Cookie 与 Bearer 两种会话来源。
          • agent-history 中所有显式错误均按日志原文归类为路径猜测/链式命令退出码/环境缺失/QA 噪声；未发现可确证归因 NocoBase3 的运行时失败，故未就三次失败推断框架缺陷。
          • repair-1、repair-3、verify-2 三轮日志极短（仅会话头），无法判断其内部意图，故仅以会话头为证、errors 记为空。
          • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。
          评审基线与版本指纹

          评审针对已封存候选补丁与当时的依赖；可能在原搭建内或之后单独补跑。与 PR 或 NocoBase3 当前最新提交不是同一概念。评审只读冻结材料，引用运行日志不代表评审者重新复现。此报告未执行最新上游源码复核。

          E3 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:124–128

          明确写出“The content route under accessPath is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.”，并给出受限文件配方：应用自行在 <accessPath>/* 安装 auth.required()，且禁止对全部路径使用 router.use('*')。

          An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

          E6 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:231–239

          说明注册表 UI 组件的用法与自定义方式、预览失败提示，以及需要用 Bearer 时如何适配，指引 Agent 找到预览组件入口。

          FileUploadField takes a repository and controlled value/onChange; onStatusChange reports idle/uploading/error so forms can prevent incomplete submissions. Accept/maxSize/maxFiles are UI checks. removeOnDelete calls deleteOne, deleting metadata only; otherwise removal unlinks the selection. ### Preview and content access

Preview supports safe raster images, PDF via a fetched blob, text/Markdown and audio/video. HTML/SVG/XML previews and unsafe URL schemes are rejected. DOCX, XLSX and PPTX use lazily loaded `@silurus/ooxml` viewers to render locally from `FileRecord.contentUrl`; their content is not sent to a third-party preview service. Legacy DOC/XLS/PPT and OpenDocument formats use Office Online, which requires an internet-accessible absolute URL and cannot use the App session.

OOXML, PDF and text fetches use same-origin credentials. An App-owned content route protected by same-origin session cookies can therefore serve restricted previews, provided it checks the caller's record access. Cross-origin fetches omit credentials and require CORS; an external cookie-protected URL is not supported by this default path. The Repository API client's Bearer token is not automatically attached to content fetches.

For Bearer-only content authorization, adapt the installed Registry source in the App. Add an authenticated content loader to the OOXML fetch path and `PreviewBody`'s PDF/text fetches, and adapt download and media/image paths as needed. Resolve authentication through the App's supported client/session services and send credentials only to the trusted content endpoint. Do not put tokens in `contentUrl`. If the adapter creates blob URLs, keep an App-owned trusted URL set, pass it to the relevant `resolveSafeFileUrl` calls, and revoke URLs when replaced or unmounted. Merely assigning a `blob:` URL to `FileRecord.contentUrl` is insufficient: the default components reject untrusted blob URLs.

          E11 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:72–78

          “Every route owns its own security”：挂载在 /api 不等于认证，未调用 auth.required() 的路由公开；要求先认证后鉴权并返回标准 401。

          ## Every route owns its own security

**Mounting under `/api` does not authenticate anything.** `/api` is a location. A route with no `auth.required()` is public regardless of where it mounts.

Never depend on middleware installed by another route, or on the order contributions happen to be registered in. Contribution order changes when a plugin is added, and a route protected only by someone else's middleware silently becomes public.

`auth.required()` rejects anonymous requests with `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`, reason `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED`. `auth.optional()` attaches the session when present without rejecting.

          原文件 SHA-256：1634e399f4f636e24d8a785ebeb6906d937a1c95b0131fff9ebaa8b15d0d1a51

          E12 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:175–177

          “Scope middleware to paths you own”：要求用显式路径或独立子 router，警告顶层 router.use('*') 会泄漏到后续贡献。

          ## Scope middleware to paths you own

Use the explicit path, or an isolated sub-router mounted at your prefix as above. A `router.use('*', ...)` on the top-level router leaks into contributions mounted later and is the way an unrelated endpoint accidentally becomes protected — or, worse, the way yours accidentally is not.

          One or two handlers belong directly in the factory. When a domain grows several handlers or shared error handling, extract a function that returns its own `Hono` and mount it:

```ts
export function createOrderRoutes(options: CreateOrderRoutesOptions): Hono {
  const routes = new Hono();
  routes.use('*', options.auth.required());
  routes.get(
    '/:orderId',
    describeRoute({
      tags: ['Orders'],
      summary: 'Get an order',
      operationId: 'getOrder',
      responses: {
        '200': dataResponse(OrderSchema),
        '401': apiErrorResponse(401),
        '404': apiErrorResponse(404),
        '500': apiErrorResponse(500),
      },
    }),
    apiValidator('param', OrderParams),
    async (context) =>
      context.json({
        data: await options.orders.get(context.req.valid('param').orderId),
      }),
  );
  return routes;
}
```

Do not write a helper that mutates a router passed in by its caller. Returning a router you own keeps the security boundary inside the thing being tested.

     * runs on — the transaction's connection when it runs in one.
     *
     * Installation data is written in Collection terms: logical field names,
     * relations and nested writes, with field encoding and timestamps handled
     * for the dialect rather than by each seed.

    repository<TRecord extends object = RepositoryRecord, TCreate extends object = Partial<TRecord>, TUpdate extends object = Partial<TRecord>>(collection: string): Repository<TRecord, TCreate, TUpdate>;
    /**

          E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:127–137

          说明 migration 面向 schema、seed 面向数据，并建议 seed 通过 context.repository 写入，query 仅在需要时使用。

          Each context carries its own tools, and which one to use is not a free choice — see [Repository and Query](#4-repository-and-query). In short: a migration works through `builder` and `query`, and a seed writes through `repository`.

The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

          原文件 SHA-256：d33a58740d6a2921689e56f964c081354574ed857389118e319719bde6936991

          E18 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:176–189

          以工具默认值表总结何时用 repository 何时用 query，并列出 query 例外的约束；未提示 select() 的泛型收窄问题。

          **Where does the code run?** Runtime code under `server/` reaches both through the manager. A migration and a seed reach both through their own context — as `context.repository(name)`, a method on the context rather than on the minimal task `connection` — but with opposite defaults, and those defaults are the rule rather than a hint:

| Context   | Tools                            | Default for data |
| --------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------- |
| Migration | `builder`, `query`, `repository` | `query`          |
| Seed      | `repository`, `query`            | `repository`     |

A migration changes structure, so `builder` and `query` are its tools and `repository` is reserved for the writes `query` would get wrong: cross-dialect field encoding and decoding, Collection-level naming overrides, and relation writes including the junction rows behind a `belongsToMany`. **Anything `query` expresses correctly stays on `query`.** A seed changes no structure and writes installation data in Collection terms, so `repository` is its normal tool and `query` covers what that cannot express, such as reading a physical table backing no Collection.

This does not loosen the self-contained rule. `repository(name)` resolves the Collection from the database itself — the metadata a previous `builder` operation wrote — which is why it is available at all; importing or iterating the application's own collection definitions from a migration stays forbidden. But a migration using it is betting on a shape it did not declare in its own body, so three constraints follow:

- Say in a comment why `query` was not enough. Nothing static can check this, and the comment is what separates a considered use from a reflex.
- Do not use it in `down`. By the time a rollback runs, the Collection has moved past what `up` left behind, possibly several migrations past.
- Do not use it to walk a table. Selecting every row and updating each one through a Repository is the shape to avoid; a set-based `query` statement is both correct and bounded.

          原文件 SHA-256：d33a58740d6a2921689e56f964c081354574ed857389118e319719bde6936991

          E19 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/protecting-routes.md:1–45

          给出在 Hono 路由上使用 auth.required()/auth.optional() 的可复制示例，说明匿名请求得到 401 UNAUTHENTICATED。

          # Protecting routes

Every server route decides its own authentication. Resolve the `Auth` instance
from the container and put `auth.required()` in front of the routes that need
a signed-in caller.

```ts
import {
  authenticationToken,
  type AuthEnv,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication';
import type { Application } from '@nocobase/app-server/application';
import {
  defineApiRoutes,
  type AppApiRouteContribution,
} from '@nocobase/app-server/router';
import { Hono } from 'hono';

export const apiRoutes: AppApiRouteContribution<Application> = defineApiRoutes(
  (app) => {
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const routes = new Hono<AuthEnv>();

    routes.use('/orders/*', auth.required());
    routes.get('/orders', (context) => {
      const { user, session } = context.get('auth')!;
      return context.json({
        data: { userId: user.id, expiresAt: session.expiresAt.toISOString() },
      });
    });

    return routes;
  },
);
```

Follow the application's own route conventions for where this file lives and
how it is registered; the application Skill's server-routes reference covers
that. This reference covers what the plugin adds.

## The two middlewares

- `auth.required()` rejects an anonymous request with `401 UNAUTHENTICATED` in the standard error body, reason `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED`, domain `authentication`. On success `context.get('auth')` is `{ user, session }`.
- Both middlewares reject a cookie-bearing write whose `Origin` (or `Referer`) is not trusted with `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`, reason `INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN`, domain `authentication`.
- `auth.optional()` sets `context.get('auth')` to the session or `null` and

          原文件 SHA-256：9a0f6f43e50640d229c848602e0a13cb3d7bd072ac0697f80c8fa885732c39b4

          E20 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/protecting-routes.md:66–90

          说明认证应先于业务授权、中间件应限定到自有前缀，并给出公开路由与测试受保护路由的做法。

          Use `getSession` directly when you have a `Request` but no Hono context, for
example in a WebSocket upgrade or a job that validates a caller-supplied
request. Do not call Better Auth's `api.getSession` yourself; it skips the
disabled check.

## Composing with authorization

Authentication answers who is calling. Authorization answers whether they may
do this. Run them in that order on the same router:

```ts
routes.use('*', auth.required(), authorization.middleware());
routes.get('/orders/:orderId', async (context) => {
  // Throws AuthorizationDeniedError, which the application answers as 403 PERMISSION_DENIED.
  await context.get('authz').require({
    resource: { type: 'orders', id: context.req.param('orderId') },
    action: 'read',
  });
  // ...
});
```

The authorization middleware reads the principal from `context.get('auth')`,
so `auth.required()` must come first. An anonymous caller gets `401` from
authentication; a signed-in caller without permission gets `403` from

          原文件 SHA-256：9a0f6f43e50640d229c848602e0a13cb3d7bd072ac0697f80c8fa885732c39b4

          E21 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:263–293

          auth.required() 实现：检查 Cookie 来源、解析会话（被拒凭据按 Better Auth APIError 状态返回），无会话时经 apiErrorHandler 返回 UNAUTHENTICATED/AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED，成功则 context.set('auth', auth)。

              required(options = {}) {
        return async (context, next) => {
            const originFailure = await this.checkCookieWriteOrigin(context);
            if (originFailure)
                return originFailure;
            if (options.skip?.(context)) {
                await next();
                return;
            }
            let auth;
            try {
                auth = await this.getSession(context.req.raw.headers);
                // A refused credential is Better Auth's APIError; answer with its status, and its code as the reason.
            }
            catch (error) {
                if (error instanceof APIError)
                    return rejectedCredential(context, error);
                throw error;
            }
            if (!auth) {
                return apiErrorHandler(new ApiError({
                    status: 'UNAUTHENTICATED',
                    reason: 'AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED',
                    domain: 'authentication',
                    message: 'Authentication required.',
                }), context);
            }
            context.set('auth', auth);
            await next();
        };
    }

          E23 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/i18n.md:3–22

          要求所有可见文案走翻译键；明确 t('projects.titel') 这类拼错不会被类型检查发现而会原样显示，由 tests/logic/app-locale-coverage.test.ts 捕获；规定先写 en-US 再补 zh-CN。

          All text a user can see goes through a translation key: titles, buttons, column headers, placeholders, `aria-label`, toasts and validation messages all count. Text hard-coded in a component cannot be translated, and changing its wording means changing code.

For the wording itself (which verb a button uses, how to write empty states and errors, how to put record names in quotes), see section C of [`../ui-guidelines.md`](../ui-guidelines.md); this document covers only how to write the code.

## Where copy lives

| File                      | Purpose                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `client/locales/en-US.ts` | English, including the sign-in pages' copy under `auth.*`; also defines the structure of the copy and exports the type `AppResource` |
| `client/locales/zh-CN.ts` | Chinese; typed as `AppResource`, so its structure must match the English                                                             |
| `client/locales/index.ts` | One dynamic `import()` per language, so the browser downloads only the language being shown                                          |

Rules:

- Each new feature gets its own group (`projects: { … }`), which holds all of the feature's copy.
- Add menu titles to the existing `navigation` group (`navigation.projects`), and write `navigation: { title: 'navigation.projects' }` in the route (see [`page.md`](page.md)).
- Add the key to `en-US.ts` first, then add the same key to `zh-CN.ts`. A key missing from `zh-CN.ts` or extra in it (including a misspelled one) fails type checking.
- The sign-in pages' copy is the flat `auth.*` keys; the authentication blocks in `client/extensions/nocobase-auth-*/` take their text as props, so `client/pages/auth/` passes these translations in.
- **Keys passed to `t()` are not type-checked.** `t('projects.titel')` compiles, and the page shows `projects.titel` as is. `tests/logic/app-locale-coverage.test.ts` catches it; run it after every copy change (its limits are in ["Checks"](#checks)).
- The English built into a few shadcn/ui primitives, such as the visually hidden "Close" of `Sheet`, is translated too (guideline C1): pass the translation as a prop where the primitive takes one, and otherwise replace the literal in the primitive with a key. ["English built into primitives" in `shadcn.md`](shadcn.md#english-built-into-primitives) lists each case.

          | Location                  | Contents                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `client/components/`      | Components shared across the whole application                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `client/components/ui/`   | shadcn primitives; add a missing one with the CLI ([`references/shadcn.md`](references/shadcn.md#1-what-the-template-ships-and-how-to-add-the-rest)); never hand-write one, and change one only as that page allows |
| `client/locales/`         | Copy: `en-US.ts`, `zh-CN.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `tests/`                  | Tests; never beside the source                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Where shared logic goes: write it in the component first. When the same logic would be copied unchanged into a second place, move it into one hook: inside one feature, a file in the feature folder (`client/pages/projects/use-project.ts`); across features, `client/hooks/` (`use-url-search.ts`). Loaders that react differently to their result, such as the detail drawer refreshing the list on a 404 while the edit dialog notifies the drawer, stay in their components, as the worked example's do. Components follow the same rule and move to `client/components/`.

          export function defineAppRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze({
        parent: 'app',
        routes: freezeNavigationRoutes(routes),
    });
}
function freezeNavigationRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze(routes.map((route) => Object.freeze({
        ...route,
        ...(route.navigation
            ? { navigation: Object.freeze({ ...route.navigation }) }
            : {}),
        ...(route.children
            ? { children: freezeNavigationRoutes(route.children) }
            : {}),
    })));
}

          /** Loads, validates, and deterministically orders seed definitions from configured sources. */
export async function loadSeeds(options) {
    const sources = normalizeSeedSources(options);
    const seeds = (await Promise.all(sources.map((source) => loadSeedSource(source)))).flat();
    validateUniqueTaskNames('Seed', seeds);
    return seeds.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

          /** Adds `auth.required()` to every path the contribution answers. */
function wrapApiContribution(
  source: AppApiRouteContribution<Application>,
): AppApiRouteContribution<Application> {
  return defineApiRoutes(async (app) => {
    const inner = await source.createRouter(app);
    const auth = resolveAuth(app);
    // Typed with Hono's default environment so the contribution stays
    // assignable to the application's router factory. `auth.required()` still
    // sets `auth` on the shared context at runtime.
    const outer = new Hono();
    outer.use(`${PROJECT_MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/*`, auth.required());
    outer.route('/', inner);
    return outer;
  });
}

          function wrapRootContribution(
  source: AppRootRouteContribution<Application>,
): AppRootRouteContribution<Application> {
  return defineRootRoutes(async (app) => {
    const inner = await source.createRouter(app);
    const auth = resolveAuth(app);
    const service = app.container.resolve<ProjectMaterialsService>(
      projectMaterialsServiceToken,
    );
    const outer = new Hono();
    outer.use(`${PROJECT_MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/*`, auth.required());
    outer.use(
      `${PROJECT_MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/*`,
      // Annotated with Hono's default `Context` so the wildcard `use` overload
      // does not leak an `any` type argument into the owner check.
      async (context: Context, next) => {
        const fileId = fileIdFromPath(context.req.path);
        if (!fileId) return context.notFound();
        const owner = await service.findFileOwner(fileId);
        if (!owner || owner.ownerId !== currentUserId(context)) {
          return context.notFound();
        }
        await next();
      },
    );
    outer.route('/', inner);
    return outer;

          function ImagePreview(inputProps: {
  readonly file: FileRecord;
  readonly url?: string;
}): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation('@nocobase/app-plugin-file');
  const { file, url } = inputProps;
  // A file can carry an image MIME type and still be damaged. The browser's
  // `onError` is the only signal available, so the failure is shown here rather
  // than leaving a broken image that looks like a successful preview.
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  if (failed) {
    return (
      <div role='alert' className='flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-8'>
        <div className='h-24 w-24'>
          <FileThumbnail file={file} />
        </div>
        <p>
          {t('files.imageFailed', {
            defaultValue:
              'This image is damaged or cannot be displayed in the browser.',
          })}
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <img
      src={url}
      alt={file.filename}
      className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );

          E36 · app/server/routes/project-materials.ts:55–120

          应用用 createProjectMaterialsRouter 组合 describeRoute、apiValidator 与 ApiError 实现材料 CRUD，显示 app-server 错误/校验契约的实际接入。

           * Runs a service write, translating a validation refusal into the standard API
 * error.
 *
 * The translation happens here rather than in `router.onError`, because the
 * application mounts every contribution with `api.route('/', router)`, which
 * merges routes into one router and drops a mounted router's own `onError`.
 * Converting before the error escapes keeps the refusal a `400`.
 */
async function runWrite<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await operation();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ProjectMaterialError) {
      throw toProjectMaterialApiError(error);
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

          What was delivered

          业务场景与交付说明

          资料与附件一体保存

          每份资料含标题（必填）与附件，PNG 显示真实图片、DOCX 在应用内渲染正文，刷新后仍在，可下载。

          保存失败可恢复

          漏填标题保存被拒且已上传附件保留，补填标题后无需重新上传即可保存成功。

          附件可移除

          在编辑表单移除附件并保存，刷新后该资料不再显示该附件。

          私有内容访问隔离

          乙与未登录访问者打开甲的资料页或附件内容链接均被拒（乙 404、未登录 401/跳登录），甲仍可正常读取。

          →
          02新建/编辑资料，填写标题并上传 PNG 或 DOCX
          →
          03保存后刷新，预览真实图片或文档正文并下载
          →
          04留空标题保存被拒，补填标题后继续保存

          07未登录访问资料页与附件链接被拒
          →
          08上传损坏 PNG 预览得到明确失败提示

          01

          甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

          Download 触发浏览器下载成功，得到 32x32 PNG，sha256=eafcdecc1468f7b18a15a7d22c494ab7e14a435f0560798ad463efc5e14f3490，与 fixtures/sample.png 完全一致，可正常打开。

          A1 · 3 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 3 张证据
          Agent 报告通过
          ⌄

          原始验收要求

          甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

          实际操作

          1. 以甲（jia@example.com / Demo123456）登录并进入 Project materials，打开 Apollo 项目启动资料 的 Edit 表单，先确认 Title 回填为原值。
          2. 在 Attachments 的 Choose files 选择 fixtures/sample.png，点击 Save 提交，出现 Saved 提示。
          3. 刷新并直接打开 /main/materials/1，点击 Preview 查看图片，再用 Download 下载附件到本地。

          观察结果

          1. 保存后详情及整页刷新后均显示 sample.png（Preview/Download），预览的 img 真实加载 naturalWidth=32，不是只显示文件名。
          2. Download 触发浏览器下载成功，得到 32x32 PNG，sha256=eafcdecc1468f7b18a15a7d22c494ab7e14a435f0560798ad463efc5e14f3490，与 fixtures/sample.png 完全一致，可正常打开。

          来源：verify-6/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

          02

          甲上传 DOCX 并保存，刷新后在应用内看到文档正文，而非只有文件名或下载按钮。

          刷新后附件仍在；重复打开预览同样渲染出文档正文。

          A2 · 2 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 2 张证据
          Agent 报告通过

          原始验收要求

          甲上传 DOCX 并保存，刷新后在应用内看到文档正文，而非只有文件名或下载按钮。

          实际操作

          1. 以甲打开 Apollo 需求评审纪要 的 Edit 表单，确认标题回填为原值后上传 fixtures/sample.docx 并点 Save。
          2. 刷新 /main/materials/2 后点击 Preview: sample.docx，等待预览渲染，并再次重复预览确认可复现。

          观察结果

          1. DOCX 预览弹窗在应用内渲染出文档正文文本 “Factory DOCX sample”，而不是只显示文件名、MIME 与下载按钮。
          2. 刷新后附件仍在；重复打开预览同样渲染出文档正文。

          来源：verify-6/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

          03

          先上传文件但留空标题，保存应失败且已选附件仍保留；补上标题后能直接保存。

          先上传文件但留空标题，保存应失败且已选附件仍保留；补上标题后能直接保存。

          ⌄

          原始验收要求

          使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。

          实际操作

          1. 以甲打开 New material，通过 Choose files 上传 fixtures/corrupt.png。
          2. 点击 Preview: corrupt.png 查看应用反馈。

          观察结果

          1. 损坏 PNG 上传被接受后，点击 Preview 弹出明确失败提示 “This image is damaged or cannot be displayed in the browser.”，没有把只显示文件名当作预览成功。
          2. 重复测试结果一致。

          来源：verify-6/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

          完整原始验收要求
          B01. 甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

B02. 甲上传 DOCX 并保存，刷新后在应用内看到文档正文，而非只有文件名或下载按钮。

B03. 先上传文件但留空标题，保存应失败且已选附件仍保留；补上标题后能直接保存。

B04. 移除一个附件并保存，刷新后这份资料不再显示该附件。

B05. 乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。

B06. 使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。
